# ContextCore.Abstractions Public API Baseline
# Format:
#   T:<FullTypeName> [(Enum|Delegate|Interface)]
#   N:<FullTypeName>+<NestedTypeName>
#   M:<FullTypeName>.<MethodSignature>
#   P:<FullTypeName>.<PropertyName> (get|set|init)
#   F:<FullTypeName>.<FieldName>
#   E:<FullTypeName>.<EventName>
# Entries are sorted alphabetically; re-running the test will produce identical output.
